- **pep**: This skill should be used when the user says "create a PEP", "update PEP", "PEP status", "document this", "spike", "investigate", "explore", "look into", "scout", "research", "scope out", "figure out", or references PEP-*.md files. Manages PEP (Project Enhancement Proposal) documents covering both investigation ("I don't know the shape yet") and execution ("here's the plan"). Wave shapes, mandatory adversarial review before execution, and evidence-required wave completion. The markdown file is the source of truth; an audit hook records what actually ran. ([denisraison/claude-plugins](https://github.com/denisraison/claude-plugins/tree/HEAD/plugins/pep/skills/pep) · ★3)
- **pep-engine**: Persistent behavioral operating system implementing the Prompt Elevation Protocol (PEP) v3.0. Governs how the agent thinks, responds, and calibrates output quality. Explicit triggers include "apply PEP", "PEP mode", "check this against PEP", "create a prompt", "enhance this prompt". The skill provides tiered complexity scaling (1-4), context ingestion engine, reasoning protocols, voice calibration, validation checks, and failure recovery mechanisms.
